- **code-reviewer**: Reviews code changes for bugs, security problems and rule violations. Use after finishing a feature, before merging. ([HT-126/e-commerce-v2](https://github.com/HT-126/e-commerce-v2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/code-reviewer.md))
- **code-reviewer**: Code-quality reviewer. After the tester passes an implementation, hand it the plan, the diff/changed files, and the test/tester reports to review for quality. Read-only — reports findings, never fixes them. Uses anthropic/claude-fable-5. ([htayj/src](https://github.com/htayj/src/blob/HEAD/dotfiles/config/.config/opencode/agents/code-reviewer.md))
- **code-reviewer**: Senior Code Reviewer. Audits source code across three dimensions — security vulnerabilities (OWASP Top 10, injection, auth flaws, secrets), refactoring opportunities (code smells, complexity, duplication, SOLID violations), and maintainability (naming, coupling, readability, test coverage). Produces a structured report with severity ratings and actionable recommendations. Use before merging a feature branch, during a code review cycle, or as a standalone audit of a module or file set. ([Htunn/Arcana](https://github.com/Htunn/Arcana/blob/HEAD/agents/code-reviewer.md))
- **code-reviewer**: Stage 7 (Review, correctness half). Reviews a PR diff against its spec.md, test-cases.md and AGENTS.md — spec drift, layering violations, missed test cases, code that disagrees with docs/. Read-only. ([hudaSharings/agentic-delivery-starter](https://github.com/hudaSharings/agentic-delivery-starter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/code-reviewer.md))
- **code-reviewer**: Reviews code before commit or deploy against the project's antipatterns, performance, query, security, and SEO rules. Activate with "review code" or "code review". ([hunter1881/CrYellowPage](https://github.com/hunter1881/CrYellowPage/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/code-reviewer.agent.md))
- **code-reviewer**: Use me to review code before commit or deploy. I review against the project's antipatterns, performance, query, security, and SEO rules. Activate me explicitly with "review code", "code review", or before any merge to main. ([hunter1881/CrYellowPage](https://github.com/hunter1881/CrYellowPage/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/code-reviewer.md))
- **code-reviewer**: Reviews code — a working-tree diff, a branch, or named files — from one focus given in its prompt (correctness, conventions, security, or tests), or from all four when no focus is given. Use once code exists and somebody has to read it back: after a change is made, before a pull request, when a user asks for a code review, or when the orchestrator reaches its Code review stage and starts one reviewer per focus. Returns APPROVED or CHANGES_REQUESTED — writes no code. ([husdeli/skills](https://github.com/husdeli/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc/agents/code-reviewer.md))

- **code-reviewer**: Peninjau kode independen untuk gate Definition of Done. Meninjau diff atau MR terhadap DoD, konvensi stack, dan kontrak API, lalu memberi putusan BERSIH atau TIDAK BERSIH dengan temuan bertingkat BLOKER, MAYOR, MINOR, INFO. Tanpa hak tulis; perbaikan dikerjakan agen pemilik wilayah. ([huzairsaputra/scrum-kit](https://github.com/huzairsaputra/scrum-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/code-reviewer.md))
